| V3-LABEL | LA | Vintage 2 is live and no surface names the vintage the record belongs to | **DONE** | FIXED 2026-08-11 - and the commissioned label was off by one on both numbers: the live vintage is 3, shadowed by 2 |  | HANDOFF_appfixes.md session 27 | 2026-08-11 | app-fixer | OUT-OF-BAND (vintage-rule surfacing, not one of the 134); build_ledger.py regenerates from the 134 audit ids only and will DROP this row. THE COMMISSIONED LABEL WAS OFF BY ONE ON BOTH NUMBERS, AND THAT IS THE FINDING, NOT A TYPO. The work was requested as 'Book vintage 2 since 2026-08-11 (capital_discipline restored); vintage 1 runs in shadow'. Measured against the register the same hour: track_meter.current_vintage() returns VINTAGE 3, opened 2026-08-11, and shadow_vintage.open_pairs() returns exactly one pair, live 3 shadowed by 2. Vintage 2 was opened by Amendment 1 on 2026-08-10 and CLOSED after ONE accrued day by the theme restoration; vintage 1 is the VOIDED run #1. So the requested string would have published a wrong vintage number and a wrong shadow on the one surface whose entire job is to say which book the numbers describe -- and it would have been wrong from the day it shipped, because the restoration had already taken the vintage. THE FIX IS THAT THE LABEL IS DERIVED, NEVER TYPED. track_meter.vintage_label() rebuilds the sentence from VINTAGES: the open vintage's number, its opening date, its own short label, and the immediately preceding vintage as the shadow. Each register row gained a <=60-char `label` field so the parenthetical also comes from the register rather than being invented at the surface. Rendered: 'Book vintage 3 since 2026-08-11 (capital_discipline restored); vintage 2 runs in shadow'. PINNED TO THE DERIVATION, NOT TO THE NUMBER. tests/test_track_meter.py already learned this the hard way -- it used to assert 'it is vintage 2', and a legitimate vintage event then failed a test that exists to catch two vintages being open at once. So no test here asserts '3'; they reconstruct the phrase from the register and compare, and a single-vintage register is mutated in to prove the no-predecessor branch emits no '; None runs in shadow' stub. TWO SOURCES FOR ONE FACT, CROSS-CHECKED RATHER THAN MERGED: track_meter answers 'which vintage is open', shadow_vintage.open_pairs() answers 'can its predecessor actually be SCORED' (it needs a pinned parameter snapshot). The label computes the predecessor from its own register -- importing shadow_vintage into track_meter would invert the module's dependency direction for a number it already holds -- and tests/test_vintage_label.py asserts the two never disagree. V1's OUTBOUND FENCE HELD AND WAS RE-ASSERTED FROM THE NEW SURFACE. test_shadow_vintage.py forbids the string 'shadow_vintage' anywhere under valuation/web or valuation/saas (PT-OUTBOUND published a research FIGURE to Discord). The label reaches the web layer via track_meter ONLY; a mutation that adds shadow_vintage to app.py's import is caught. The label also carries NO measurement -- no return, excess or paired difference -- pinned by a test that rejects float values and measurement-shaped keys. Naming a vintage is bookkeeping; publishing its paired difference is the thing the fence exists to stop. OWNER-ONLY, AND THAT IS LOAD-BEARING: the label ships on /api/index-track and, via paper_track's contract_track block, on /api/track -- both in surfaces.OWNER_ONLY_PATHS, pinned by a test, because the argument that this is safe to render rests on it. PUBLIC POSTURE UNTOUCHED, pinned: the backtested/live gate still reads `long_enough = days >= MIN_LIVE_DAYS` (recorded rows, LA8's rule), and a mutation adding the vintage to landing.html is caught. SECOND HALF OF THE BRIEF -- THE THEME LEGEND -- AND THE ANSWER IS SPLIT. THE BARS WERE ALWAYS DATA-DRIVEN and picked the fifth theme up on their own: _themeBars enumerates Object.entries(w) over whatever weights the payload carries, and health.theme_contributing (the 'themes not driving this scan' warning) is computed server-side over settings.FACTORS_ALL. Confirmed by running them, not by reading them. WHAT WAS HARDCODED WAS THE CAPTION UNDER THE BAR, and on 2026-08-11 it was wrong on TWO counts about the one theme the whole day was about: THEME_INPUTS.capital_discipline read 'low share issuance . low asset growth (dormant - needs data)'. It was NOT dormant -- it had just become the fifth live theme, the adoption that opened vintage 3 -- and factors.py:265 computes the theme as df[['z_neg_issuance']].mean(axis=1), ISSUANCE ONLY, asset growth having been deliberately removed for cancelling out the one input that works. A confident wrong caption is worse than a missing bar: a missing bar invites a question, a caption closes one. FIXED BY MOVING THE COPY TO PYTHON, following the score_confidence.py / hold_horizon.py convention already in the tree: valuation/web/theme_status.py is injected as window.THEME_STATUS and app.js only escapes it. Dormancy is now rendered as its own flagged line rather than folded into the ingredient list. The dead themes say WHY: institutional and insider FAILED the PREREG_theme_restoration.md fidelity gate (Spearman +0.17 and +0.36 against 0.60) -- 'needs data' would have implied the fix is a download, and it is not; low_risk is described as zeroed on evidence, not missing. DELIBERATELY NOT DERIVED FROM A LIVE SCAN: health.theme_contributing measures what survived standardization TODAY and is the right instrument for the scan-health warning, but issuance.py fails to None on an SEC outage by design, so driving the legend off it would make a transient outage read as a retired theme. The legend states the DESIGN, the health block states the DAY. 30 tests (16 vintage-label, 14 theme-status); 19/19 mutations caught, 0 missed, 0 skipped -- including a first pass where 6 SKIPPED silently because multi-line anchors were written with \n against CRLF files and one reason text carries an em dash, which is how a pin gets credited without ever being exercised. ZERO RESEARCH TRIALS -- a labelling and copy fix, no measurement, no scoring change, so NO VINTAGE EVENT: equity N unchanged. |

THE TAB'S OWN LIVE SUB-SCORES ARE NOT COMPUTABLE POINT-IN-TIME (quality needs a WACC and S23 measured that path fetching live Yahoo prices to value 1999), so the register committed an ASYMMETRY IN ADVANCE: a NULL is informative because the arm used the panel's strongest theme at IC t +3.10, but a POSITIVE would NOT have licensed the tab's copy without a separate live-vs-panel fidelity check.
